reviewer_name: Renee Nolan
rating: 4
body: Great amenities, however the saunas are in the restrooms so you are separate from your partner if of opposite sex. The two hot tubs are indoors and are small (four adults max) fiberglass tubs but nice and warm. The doors whistle like crazy on a windy day. The lobby is nice for gatherings with a large TV and view of pool and beach.  Very clean and maintenance/desk staff are very efficient and friendly. Elevators can be slow. Exit to road is blocked by our condo so we have to travel through two other parking lots to exit. Overall would stay here again.

id: b434987d-6d20-4c5c-8947-4050271e7938
entity_slug: phoenix-i
reviewer_name: Amanda McGowen
rating: 5
body: We had the best time! We stayed at Phoenix III a few years ago and did not have the best experience. Stayed at Phoenix I since it was a last minute trip and had availability and honestly we had no complaints! Security was rounding constantly and when we did have an issue with kids throwing ice from the penthouse it was quickly addressed. I’m sure the kids didn’t think anything of it but ice falling from that high with small kids in the pool could have been a serious issue if it had hit one of them. Our room was clean upon arrival. Elevators were quick. The entire condo wasn’t overcrowded at all which was amazing being just 2 days after the 4th! My husband even saw maintenance firing up the grills and cleaning them which we have never seen at any resort. I highly recommend for families!
